# Excalidraw Data

## Text Elements
CREATE VIEW student_grades(ID, GPA) AS
    SELECT ID, SUM(grade_points.points*course.credits)/SUM(course.credits) as GPA 
    FROM takes 
    -- gop bang theo course id
    JOINS course ON takes.course_id = course.course_id
    -- gop bang grade, quy doi diem chu sang diem so
    JOINS grade_points ON takes.grade = grade_points.grade
    WHERE grade_points.grade IS NOT NULL; ^05eeQPBB
